- **handoff**: Save where the project stands so the next session — yours, another tool's, or another person's — can resume without re-establishing context. Use when wrapping up a work session, before switching between Claude Code and Cowork, before a break, or when the user says handoff, write a handoff, hand this off, checkpoint, wrap up, save state, tidy the docs, or where did we leave off. - **handoff-check**: When the user greenlights a substantial, multi-step task, reflects on whether the current context is too full or too off-topic to run it efficiently, and — only if a clean context would be clearly cheaper and safer — recommends a handoff (fresh session or subagent) with a ready-to-paste brief and the reasoning. - **handover**: Generate a session handover brief before /clear.
